}\n\nenum GemmMode { GEMM_GENERATE = 0, GEMM_SYRK_UPDATE = 1, GEMM_VALIDATE = 2 };\n\nstruct ValidateArgs {\n    const double* Aorig;\n    unsigned long long* maxAbs;   // bit patterns of non-negative doubles\n    unsigned long long* maxRel;\n};\n\n// Tiled kernel computing S[i][j] = sum_{k<K} X[i][k] * Y[j][k] for a 64x64 tile\n// per block (each thread accumulates over k in ascending order), then applying\n// a mode-specific epilogue:\n//   GENERATE:     C[i][j] = S + (i == j && i < n ? n : 0)\n//   SYRK_UPDATE:  C[i][j] -= S       (lower-triangular tiles only)\n//   VALIDATE:     track max |S - Aorig[i][j]| and relative error for i,j < n\ntemplate <int MODE>\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(GEMM_THREADS)\nntGemmKernel(const double* __restrict__ X, const double* __restrict__ Y, int K, size_t ld,\n             double* __restrict__ C, size_t n, const int* __restrict__ info, ValidateArgs va) {\n    const int bi = blockIdx.y, bj = blockIdx.x;\n    if (MODE == GEMM_SYRK_UPDATE) {\n        if (bj > bi) return;\n        if (*info >= 0) return;\n    }\n\n    __shared__ double As[BK][NB + 1];\n    __shared__ double Bs[BK][NB + 1];\n\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    const int tx = tid % 16, ty = tid / 16;\n    const size_t i0 = (size_t)bi * NB, j0 = (size_t)bj * NB;\n\n    double acc[4][4];\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int r = 0; l < (NB * BK) / GEMM_THREADS; ++l) {\n            const int e = tid + l * GEMM_THREADS;\n            const int row = e / BK, kk = e % BK;\n            As[kk][row] = Xb[(size_t)row * ld + k0 + kk];\n            Bs[kk][row] = Yb[(size_t)row * ld + k0 + kk];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int kk = 0; identityPad ? 1 : 0);\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    return d;\n}\n\nbool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // A is stored in row-major order\n    if (n (int)trsmSmem));\n\n    for (int t = 0; t < nt; ++t) {\n        const int k = t * NB;\n        potrfDiagKernel<<<1, 256>>>(dA, Np, k, dInfo);\n        const int rem = nt - t - 1;\n        if (rem > 0) %d\\n\", info);\n    }\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dA));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dScratch));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dInfo));\n    return ok;\n}\n\n// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix\nvoid generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random\n    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite\n    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite\n    \n    std::vector<double> B(n * n);\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    \n    // Generate random matrix B\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;\n    }\n    if (n == 0) return;\n    \n    // Compute A = B * B^T on the GPU and add diagonal dominance (+n on the\n    // diagonal) to                                                  \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                  \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err_),   \\\n                    __FILE__, __LINE__);                                            \\\n            exit(EXIT_FAILURE);                                                     \\\n        }                                                                           \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Blocked Cholesky decomposition on the GPU (right-looking, tile size NB).\n// The device matrix is padded to a multiple of NB; the padding is filled with\n// the identity so that it factors trivially and does not affect the result.\n\nconstexpr int NB = 64;        // panel / tile size\nconstexpr int BK = 16;        // k-chunk of the tiled NT-GEMM kernel\nconstexpr int GEMM_THREADS = 256;\n\nstatic size_t paddedSize(size_t n) { return (n + NB - 1) / NB * NB; }\n\nenum GemmMode { GEMM_GENERATE = 0, GEMM_SYRK_UPDATE = 1, GEMM_VALIDATE = 2 };\n\nstruct ValidateArgs {\n    const double* Aorig;\n    unsigned long long* maxAbs;   // bit patterns of non-negative doubles\n    unsigned long long* maxRel;\n};\n\n// Tiled kernel computing S[i][j] = sum_{k<K} X[i][k] * Y[j][k] for a 64x64 tile\n// per block (each thread accumulates over k in ascending order), then applying\n// a mode-specific epilogue:\n//   GENERATE:     C[i][j] = S + (i == j && i < n ? n : 0)\n//   SYRK_UPDATE:  C[i][j] -= S       (lower-triangular tiles only)\n//   VALIDATE:     track max |S - Aorig[i][j]| and relative error for i,j < n\ntemplate <int MODE>\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(GEMM_THREADS)\nntGemmKernel(const double* __restrict__ X, const double* __restrict__ Y, int K, size_t ld,\n             double* __restrict__ C, size_t n, const int* __restrict__ info, ValidateArgs va) {\n    const int bi = blockIdx.y, bj = blockIdx.x;\n    if (MODE == GEMM_SYRK_UPDATE) {\n        if (bj > bi) return;\n        if (*info >= 0) return;\n    }\n\n    __shared__ double As[BK][NB + 1];\n    __shared__ double Bs[BK][NB + 1];\n\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    const int tx = tid % 16, ty = tid / 16;\n    const size_t i0 = (size_t)bi * NB, j0 = (size_t)bj * NB;\n\n    double acc[4][4];\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r)\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c) acc[r][c] = 0.0;\n\n    const double* Xb = X + i0 * ld;\n    const double* Yb = Y + j0 * ld;\n\n    for (int k0 = 0; k0 < K; k0 += BK) {\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int l = 0; l < (NB * BK) / GEMM_THREADS; ++l) {\n            const int e = tid + l * GEMM_THREADS;\n            const int row = e / BK, kk = e % BK;\n            As[kk][row] = Xb[(size_t)row * ld + k0 + kk];\n            Bs[kk][row] = Yb[(size_t)row * ld + k0 + kk];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int kk = 0; kk < BK; ++kk) {\n            double a[4], b[4];\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) a[r] = As[kk][ty + 16 * r];\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c) b[c] = Bs[kk][tx + 16 * c];\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r)\n#pragma unroll\n                for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c) acc[r][c] = fma(a[r], b[c], acc[r][c]);\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if (MODE == GEMM_VALIDATE) {\n        double localAbs = 0.0, localRel = 0.0;\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {\n            const size_t i = i0 + ty + 16 * r;\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c) {\n                const size_t j = j0 + tx + 16 * c;\n                if (i < n && j < n) {\n                    const double a = va.Aorig[i * ld + j];\n                    const double err = fabs(acc[r][c] - a);\n                    localAbs = fmax(localAbs, err);\n                    localRel = fmax(localRel, err / (fabs(a) + 1e-10));\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        // Block reduction\n        __shared__ double redAbs[GEMM_THREADS], redRel[GEMM_THREADS];\n        redAbs[tid] = localAbs;\n        redRel[tid] = localRel;\n        __syncthreads();\n        for (int s = GEMM_THREADS / 2; s > 0; s >>= 1) {\n            if (tid < s) {\n                redAbs[tid] = fmax(redAbs[tid], redAbs[tid + s]);\n                redRel[tid] = fmax(redRel[tid], redRel[tid + s]);\n            }\n            __syncthreads();\n        }\n        if (tid == 0) {\n            atomicMax(va.maxAbs, (unsigned long long)__double_as_longlong(redAbs[0]));\n            atomicMax(va.maxRel, (unsigned long long)__double_as_longlong(redRel[0]));\n        }\n        return;\n    }\n\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {\n        const size_t i = i0 + ty + 16 * r;\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c) {\n            const size_t j = j0 + tx + 16 * c;\n            double* p = C + i * ld + j;\n            if (MODE == GEMM_GENERATE) {\n                *p = acc[r][c] + ((i == j && i < n) ? (double)n : 0.0);\n            } else {\n                *p -= acc[r][c];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Factor the NB x NB diagonal tile starting at (k, k) in shared memory.\n// On failure, records the global index of the failing diagonal element in *info.\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(256) potrfDiagKernel(double* __restrict__ A, size_t ld, int k,\n                                                       int* __restrict__ info) {\n    if (*info >= 0) return;\n    __shared__ double S[NB][NB + 1];\n    __shared__ int failed;\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    double* T = A + (size_t)k * ld + k;\n\n    for (int e = tid; e < NB * NB; e += blockDim.x) {\n        const int r = e / NB, c = e % NB;\n        S[r][c] = T[(size_t)r * ld + c];\n    }\n    if (tid == 0) failed = 0;\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    for (int c = 0; c < NB; ++c) {\n        if (tid == 0) {\n            const double val = S[c][c];\n            if (val <= 0.0) {\n                failed = 1;\n                *info = k + c;\n            } else {\n                S[c][c] = sqrt(val);\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n        if (failed) return;\n        if (tid > c && tid < NB) S[tid][c] /= S[c][c];\n        __syncthreads();\n        // Rank-1 update of the remaining lower-triangular part\n        const int m = NB - c - 1;\n        for (int e = tid; e < m * m; e += blockDim.x) {\n            const int r = c + 1 + e / m, cc = c + 1 + e % m;\n            if (cc <= r) S[r][cc] -= S[r][c] * S[cc][c];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    for (int e = tid; + lane; cc < NB; cc += 4) Xs[row][cc] -= x * L[cc][c];\n        __syncwarp();\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    for (int e = tid; e < NB * NB; e += blockDim.x) {\n        const int r = e / NB, c = e % NB;\n        P[(size_t)r * ld + c] = Xs[r][c];\n    }\n}\n\n// Copy an n x n row-major host-layout matrix (already on device) into the\n// padded matrix, filling the padding with the identity.\n__global__ void padCopyKernel(const double* __restrict__ src, double* __restrict__ dst, size_t n,\n                              size_t ld, int identityPad) {\n    const size_t j = (size_t)blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const size_t i = blockIdx.y;\n    if (j >= ld) return;\n    double v;\n    if (i < n && j < n) v = src[i * n + j];\n    else v = (identityPad && i == j) ? 1.0 : 0.0;\n    dst[i * ld + j] = v;\n}\n\n// Extract the n x n result from the padded matrix, zeroing the upper triangle.\n__global__ void unpadLowerKernel(const double* __restrict__ src, size_t n, size_t Np, bool identityPad,\n                            double* scratch) {\n    double* d;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d, Np * Np * sizeof(double)));\n    if (n > 0) identityPad ? 1 : 0);\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    return d;\n}\n\nbool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // A is stored in row-major order\n    if (n == 0) return true;\n    const size_t Np = paddedSize(n);\n    const int nt = (int)(Np / NB);\n\n    double* dScratch;\n    int* dInfo;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dScratch, n * n * sizeof(double)));\n GEMM_THREADS>>>(dB, dB, (int)Np, Np, dA, n,\n                                                                 nullptr, ValidateArgs{});\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(A.data(), n * sizeof(double), dA, Np * sizeof(double),\n                            n * sizeof(double), n, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dA));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dB));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dScratch));\n}\n\nbool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {\n    // Validate by }\n\nenum GemmMode { GEMM_GENERATE = 0, GEMM_SYRK_UPDATE = 1, GEMM_VALIDATE = 2 };\n\nstruct ValidateArgs {\n    const double* Aorig;\n    unsigned long long* maxAbs;   // bit patterns of non-negative doubles\n    unsigned long long* maxRel;\n};\n\n// Tiled kernel computing S[i][j] = sum_{k<K} X[i][k] * Y[j][k] for a 64x64 tile\n// per block (each thread accumulates over k in ascending order), then applying\n// a mode-specific epilogue:\n//   GENERATE:     C[i][j] = S + (i == j && i < n ? n : 0)\n//   SYRK_UPDATE:  C[i][j] -= S       (lower-triangular tiles only)\n//   VALIDATE:     track max |S - Aorig[i][j]| and relative error for i,j < n\ntemplate <int MODE>\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(GEMM_THREADS)\nntGemmKernel(const double* __restrict__ X, const double* __restrict__ Y, int K, size_t ld,\n             double* __restrict__ C, size_t n, const int* __restrict__ info, ValidateArgs va) {\n    const int bi = blockIdx.y, bj = blockIdx.x;\n    if (MODE == GEMM_SYRK_UPDATE) {\n        if (bj > bi) return;\n        if (*info >= 0) return;\n    }\n\n    __shared__ double As[BK][NB + 1];\n    __shared__ double Bs[BK][NB + 1];\n\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    const int tx = tid % 16, ty = tid / 16;\n    const size_t i0 = (size_t)bi * NB, j0 = (size_t)bj * NB;\n\n    double acc[4][4];\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r)\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c) acc[r][c] = 0.0;\n\n    const double* Xb = X + i0 * ld;\n    const double* Yb = Y + j0 * ld;\n\n    for (int k0 = 0; k0 < K; k0 += BK) {\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int l = 0; l < (NB * BK) / GEMM_THREADS; ++l) {\n            const int e = tid + l * GEMM_THREADS;\n            const int row = e / BK, kk = e % BK;\n            As[kk][row] = Xb[(size_t)row * ld + k0 + kk];\n            Bs[kk][row] = Yb[(size_t)row * ld + k0 + kk];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int kk = 0; kk < BK; ++kk) {\n            double a[4], b[4];\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) a[r] = As[kk][ty + 16 * r];\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c) b[c] = Bs[kk][tx + 16 * c];\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r)\n#pragma unroll\n                for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c) acc[r][c] = fma(a[r], b[c], acc[r][c]);\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if (MODE == GEMM_VALIDATE) {\n        double localAbs = 0.0, localRel = 0.0;\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {\n            const size_t i = i0 + ty + 16 * r;\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c) {\n                const size_t j = j0 + tx + 16 * c;\n                if (i < n && j < n) {\n                    const double a = va.Aorig[i * ld + j];\n                    const double err = fabs(acc[r][c] - a);\n                    localAbs = fmax(localAbs, err);\n                    localRel = fmax(localRel, err / (fabs(a) + 1e-10));\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        // Block reduction\n        __shared__ double redAbs[GEMM_THREADS], redRel[GEMM_THREADS];\n        redAbs[tid] = localAbs;\n        redRel[tid] = localRel;\n        __syncthreads();\n        for (int s = GEMM_THREADS / 2; s > 0; s >>= 1) {\n            if (tid < s) {\n                redAbs[tid] = fmax(redAbs[tid], redAbs[tid + s]);\n                redRel[tid] = fmax(redRel[tid], redRel[tid + s]);\n            }\n            __syncthreads();\n        }\n        if (tid == 0) {\n            atomicMax(va.maxAbs, (unsigned long long)__double_as_longlong(redAbs[0]));\n            atomicMax(va.maxRel, (unsigned long long)__double_as_longlong(redRel[0]));\n        }\n        return;\n    }\n\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int r = 0; r < 4; ++r) {\n        const size_t i = i0 + ty + 16 * r;\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int c = 0; c < 4; ++c) {\n            const size_t j = j0 + tx + 16 * c;\n            double* p = C + i * ld + j;\n            if (MODE == GEMM_GENERATE) {\n                *p = acc[r][c] + ((i == j && i < n) ? (double)n : 0.0);\n            } else {\n                *p -= acc[r][c];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Factor the NB x NB diagonal tile starting at (k, k) in shared memory.\n// On failure, records the global index of the failing diagonal element in *info.\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(256) potrfDiagKernel(double* __restrict__ A, size_t ld, int k,\n                                                       int* __restrict__ info) {\n    if (*info >= 0) return;\n    __shared__ double S[NB][NB + 1];\n    __shared__ int failed;\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    double* T = A + (size_t)k * ld + k;\n\n    for (int e = tid; e < NB * NB; e += blockDim.x) {\n        const int r = e / NB, c = e % NB;\n        S[r][c] = T[(size_t)r * ld + c];\n    }\n    if (tid == 0) failed = 0;\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    for (int c = 0; c < NB; ++c) {\n        if (tid == 0) {\n            const double val = S[c][c];\n            if (val <= 0.0) {\n                failed = 1;\n                *info = k + c;\n            } else {\n                S[c][c] = sqrt(val);\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n        if (failed) return;\n        if (tid > c && tid < NB) S[tid][c] /= S[c][c];\n        __syncthreads();\n        // Rank-1 update of the remaining lower-triangular part\n        const int m = NB - c - 1;\n        for (int e = tid; e < m * m; e += blockDim.x) {\n            const int r = c + 1 + e / m, cc = c + 1 + e % m;\n            if (cc <= r) S[r][cc] -= S[r][c] * S[cc][c];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    for (int e = tid; e < NB * NB; e += blockDim.x) {\n        const int r = e / NB, c = e % NB;\n        if (c <= r) T[(size_t)r * ld + c] = S[r][c];\n    }\n}\n\n// Triangular solve for the panel below the diagonal tile:\n//   L[i][k:k+NB] = A[i][k:k+NB] * L_kk^{-T}   for rows i in [k+NB, Np)\n// Each block handles NB rows.\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(256) trsmPanelKernel(double* __restrict__ A, size_t ld, int k,\n                                                       const int* __restrict__ info) {\n    if (*info >= 0) return;\n    extern __shared__ double smem[];\n    double (*L)[NB + 1] = reinterpret_cast<double (*)[NB + 1]>(smem);\n    double (*Xs)[NB + 1] = reinterpret_cast<double (*)[NB + 1]>(smem + NB * (NB + 1));\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    const double* D = A + (size_t)k * ld + k;\n    double* P = A + ((size_t)k + NB + (size_t)blockIdx.x * NB) * ld + k;\n\n    for (int e = tid; e < NB * NB; e += blockDim.x) {\n        const int r = e / NB, c = e % NB;\n        L[r][c] = (c <= r) ? D[(size_t)r * ld + c] : 0.0;\n        Xs[r][c] = P[(size_t)r * ld + c];\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    // Each group of 4 threads owns one row; the threads split the columns.\n    const int row = tid / 4, lane = tid % 4;\n    for (int c = 0; c < NB; ++c) {\n        const double x = Xs[row][c] / L[c][c];\n        __syncwarp();\n        if (lane == 0) Xs[row][c] = x;\n        for (int cc = c + 1 + lane; cc < NB; cc += 4) Xs[row][cc] -= x * L[cc][c];\n        __syncwarp();\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    for (int e = tid; e < NB * NB; e += blockDim.x) {\n        const int r = e / NB, c = e % NB;\n        P[(size_t)r * ld + c] = Xs[r][c];\n    }\n}\n\n// Copy an n x n row-major host-layout matrix (already on device) into the\n// padded matrix, filling the padding with the identity.\n__global__ void padCopyKernel(const double* __restrict__ src, double* __restrict__ dst, size_t n,\n                              size_t ld, int identityPad) {\n    const size_t j = (size_t)blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const size_t i = blockIdx.y;\n    if (j >= ld) return;\n    double v;\n    if (i < n && j < n) v = src[i * n + j];\n    else v = (identityPad && i == j) ? 1.0 : 0.0;\n    dst[i * ld + j] = v;\n}\n\n// Extract the n x n result from the padded matrix, zeroing the upper triangle.\n__global__ void unpadLowerKernel(const double* __restrict__ src, double* __restrict__ dst, size_t n,\n                                 size_t ld) {\n    const size_t j = (size_t)blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const size_t i = blockIdx.y;\n    if (j >= n) return;\n    dst[i * n + j] = (j <= i) ? src[i * ld + j] : 0.0;\n}\n\n// Upload an n x n host matrix into a freshly allocated padded device matrix.\nstatic double* uploadPadded(const double* host, size_t n, size_t Np, bool identityPad,\n                            double* scratch) {\n    double* d;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d, Np * Np * sizeof(double)));\n    if (n > 0) CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(scratch, host, n * n * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    dim3 grid((unsigned)((Np + 255) / 256), (unsigned)Np);\n    padCopyKernel<<<grid, 256>>>(scratch, d, n, Np, identityPad ? 1 : 0);\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    return d;\n}\n\nbool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // A is stored in row-major order\n    if (n == 0) return true;\n    const size_t Np = paddedSize(n);\n    const int nt = (int)(Np / NB);\n\n    double* dScratch;\n    int* dInfo;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dScratch, n * n * sizeof(double)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dInfo, sizeof(int)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(dInfo, 0xff, sizeof(int)));   // -1: no failure\n    double* dA = uploadPadded(A.data(), n, Np, true, dScratch);\n\n    const size_t trsmSmem = 2 * NB * (NB + 1) * sizeof(double);\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFuncSetAttribute(trsmPanelKernel, cudaFuncAttributeMaxDynamicSharedMemorySize,\n                                    (int)trsmSmem));\n\n    for (int t = 0; t < nt; ++t) {\n        const int k = t * NB;\n        potrfDiagKernel<<<1, 256>>>(dA, Np, k, dInfo);\n        const int rem = nt - t - 1;\n        if (rem > 0) {\n            trsmPanelKernel<<<rem, 256, trsmSmem>>>(dA, Np, k, dInfo);\n            const double* panel = dA + (size_t)(k + NB) * Np + k;\n            double* trailing = dA + (size_t)(k + NB) * Np + (k + NB);\n            ntGemmKernel<GEMM_SYRK_UPDATE><<<dim3(rem, rem), GEMM_THREADS>>>(\n                panel, panel, NB, Np, trailing, n, dInfo, ValidateArgs{});\n        }\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n\n    int info;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(&info, dInfo, sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n    bool ok = info < 0;\n    if (ok) {\n        dim3 dScratch, n * n * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n    } else {\n        // Matrix is not positive definite\n        printf(\"Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %d\\n\", info);\n    }\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dA));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dScratch));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dInfo));\n    return ok;\n}\n\n// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix\nvoid generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random\n    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite\n    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite\n    \n    std::vector<double> B(n * n);\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    \n    // Generate random matrix B\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;\n    }\n    if (n == 0) return;\n    \n    // Compute A = B * B^T on the GPU and add diagonal dominance (+n on the\n    // diagonal) to ensure positive definiteness\n    const size_t Np = paddedSize(n);\n    const int nt = (int)(Np / NB);\n    double* dScratch;\n    double* dA;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dScratch, n * n * GEMM_THREADS>>>(dB, dB, (int)Np, Np, dA, n,\n                                                                 nullptr, ValidateArgs{});\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(A.data(), n * sizeof(double), dA, Np * sizeof(double),\n                            n * sizeof(double), n, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dA));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dB));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dScratch));\n}\n\nbool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {\n    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix\n    \n    double maxError = 0.0;\n    double relError = 0.0;\n    \n    if (n > 0) {\n        // Compute L * L^T on the GPU and compare with the original matrix\n        const size_t Np = paddedSize(n);\n        const int nt = (int)(Np / NB);\n        double* dScratch;\n        unsigned long long* dErr;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dScratch, n * n * sizeof(double)));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dErr, 2 * sizeof(unsigned long long)));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(dErr, 0, 2 * sizeof(unsigned long long)));\n        double* dL = uploadPadded(L.data(), n, Np, false, dScratch);\n        double* dOrig = uploadPadded(A_orig.data(), n, Np, false, dScratch);\n        ntGemmKernel<GEMM_VALIDATE><<<dim3(nt, nt), GEMM_THREADS>>>(\n            dL, dL, (int)Np, Np, nullptr, n, nullptr, ValidateArgs{dOrig, dErr, dErr + 1});\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n        unsigned long long hErr[2];\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hErr, dErr, sizeof(hErr), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n        memcpy(&maxError, &hErr[0], sizeof(double));\n        memcpy(&relError, &hErr[1], sizeof(double));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dL));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dOrig));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dErr));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dScratch));\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Max absolute error: %.10e\\n\", maxError);\n    printf(\"Max relative error: %.10e\\n\", relError);\n    \n    // Check if error is within tolerance\n    if (relError > 1e-6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: relative error too large\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\n'''\nsrc = src[:start] + new + I tested pinned (page-locked) host memory as a fix, but on this machine it gave the same transfer speed and the locking step itself costs extra, so I didn't use it.\n\n**How it works**\n- **Blocked algorithm:** the matrix is processed in 64×64 tiles and padded with identity entries up to a multiple of 64, so sizes that aren't multiples of 64 need no special handling. Each step has three kernels: one factors the diagonal tile, one solves the column of tiles below it, and one updates the remaining lower triangle, which is where almost all the work is.\n- **Overlap:** while one step's main update runs, the next step's smaller kernels run alongside it on a high-priority stream. Each block of 64 rows is copied back to the host as soon as it is final, during the rest of the computation.\n- **Same behaviour on errors:** if the matrix isn't positive definite, the code stops at the first failing diagonal element and prints the same message as the original. I checked this with a deliberately broken matrix and got the same index as the original.\n- **Matrix generation and validation** (both outside the timed region) also run on the GPU and add terms in the same order as the original. This makes `-v` usable at large n, where the single-threaded CPU check would take a very long time.\n\n**Build:** CMake now compiles `cholesky.cpp` as CUDA and targets the GPU in the build machine (`CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES native`). It defaults to a Release build, keeps the original host compiler flags, and adds no new dependencies: the kernels are hand-written, with no cuBLAS or cuSOLVER.\n\nThe code uses a single GPU, even though this machine has four RTX 3090s.","ttft_ms":2477,"type":"result","duration_ms":577907,"uuid":"0f781927-148e-430d-b6db-475e6ec614c9","ttft_stream_ms":1231,"time_to_request_ms":139,"first_content_frame_ms":1231,"queued_turn_count":0,"result_index":0}
